y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 *         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz;                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};\n    \n    printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n    printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n    printf(\"Elements: %zu\\n\", N);\n    printf(\"Sum: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(sum));\n    printf(\"Min: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(min_val));\n    printf(\"Max: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(max_val));\n    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {\n        printf(\"Sample[%zu]: %.17e\\n\", exact hash comparison)\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgoHt87vkrB3jqabKZx8h","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Rb4AV3fHCsKBsHqSkANrpJ","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp <<'EOF'\n// Cahn-Hilliard phase separation benchmark\n//\n// Hybrid parallelization:\n//   * MPI    : 1D slab decomposition of the 3D grid along Z, one rank per GPU,\n//              halo exchange of boundary planes every half step, overlapped with\n//              the interior stencil computation on the GPU.\n//   * CUDA   : the chemical-potential and concentration-update stencils run on\n//              the GPU (register-marching 7-point stencil kernels, two streams).\n//   * OpenMP : host-side work (field initialization, halo buffer handling,\n//              gather and validation reductions) is multithreaded.\n//\n// Semantics (initial field, stencil, clamped boundaries, update, output) are\n// identical to the original serial implementation.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,          \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                                 \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                       \\\n        }                                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                                         \\\n    do {                                                                                        \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d (code %d)\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,     \\\n                    err__);                                                                     \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                       \\\n        }                                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,\n                                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n//\n// Local storage layout on each rank: planes 0 .. nzl+1, where plane 0 and\n// plane nzl+1 are halo planes received from the neighbouring ranks and planes\n// 1 .. nzl hold the rank's own slab (local plane k is stored at k+1).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\nconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 8;   // planes marched per thread along z\n\nstruct StencilParams {\n    int nx, ny, nzl;          // local slab dimensions (nzl = own planes)\n    int has_prev, has_next;   // 1 if a neighbour rank exists in that z direction\n    double idx2, idy2, idz2;  // 1/dx^2, 1/dy^2, 1/dz^2\n};\n\n// Laplacian of f at (x, y, storage plane zs) with clamped boundaries.\n// zn / zp are the storage planes of the z neighbours (already clamped).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(const double* __restrict__ f, const StencilParams p,\n                                            const int x, const int y, const int zs,\n                                            const int zn, const int zp,\n                                            const double center) {\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(zs) * plane;\n\n    const double cxx = (f[base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + xp] +\n                        f[base + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + xn] - 2.0 * center) * p.idx2;\n    const double cyy = (f[base + static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] +\n                        f[base + static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] - 2.0 * center) * p.idy2;\n    const double czz = (f[static_cast<size_t>(zp) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x] +\n                        f[static_cast<size_t>(zn) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x] -\n                        2.0 * center) * p.idz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on local planes [z0, z1)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\nchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n                        const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n                        const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n        const int zs = zl + 1;\n        const int zn = (zl > 0 || p.has_prev) ? zs - 1 : zs;\n        const int zp = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next) ? zs + 1 : zs;\n        const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(zs) * plane + off;\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                  - gamma * laplacian(c, p, x, y, zs, zn, zp, cv);\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu) on local planes [z0, z1)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\nupdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n             const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n             const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n        const int zs = zl + 1;\n        const int zn = (zl > 0 || p.has_prev) ? zs - 1 : zs;\n        const int zp = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next) ? zs + 1 : zs;\n        const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(zs) * plane + off;\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * laplacian(mu, p, x, y, zs, zn, zp, mu[idx]);\n    }\n}\n\ninline dim3 gridFor(const StencilParams& p, const int z0, const int z1) {\n    const int nz = std::max(0, z1 - z0);\n    return dim3((p.nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X, (p.ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,\n                (nz + Z_CHUNK - 1) / Z_CHUNK);\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Distributed simulation state\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstruct Domain {\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    size_t nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;\n    int nzl = 0;         // own planes on this rank\n    size_t z_off = 0;    // global index of first own plane\n    int prev = MPI_PROC_NULL, next = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    std::vector<int> counts, displs;  // planes per rank / first plane (for gather)\n\n    size_t plane() const { return nx * ny; }\n    size_t localElems() const { return plane() * nzl; }\n    size_t storageElems() const { return plane() * (nzl + 2); }\n};\n\nstatic Domain makeDomain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Domain d;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size));\n    d.nx = nx; d.ny = ny; d.nz = nz;\n    d.counts.resize(d.size);\n    d.displs.resize(d.size);\n    size_t acc = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n        const size_t cnt = nz / d.size + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < nz % d.size ? 1 : 0);\n        d.counts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n        d.displs[r] = static_cast<int>(acc);\n        acc += cnt;\n    }\n    d.nzl = d.counts[d.rank];\n    d.z_off = d.displs[d.rank];\n    // Nearest neighbours that actually own planes (ranks with 0 planes are skipped)\n    for (int r = d.rank - 1; r >= 0; --r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.prev = r; break; }\n    for (int r = d.rank + 1; r < d.size; ++r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.next = r; break; }\n    return d;\n}\n\n// Halo exchange of the first / last own plane of `field` with the neighbouring\n// ranks. D2H/H2D copies are staged through pinned host buffers on `stream`.\nstruct HaloExchanger {\n    const Domain* d = nullptr;\n    double* h_send_lo = nullptr;  // own first plane  -> prev\n    double* h_send_hi = nullptr;  // own last plane   -> next\n    double* h_recv_lo = nullptr;  // from prev -> halo plane 0\n    double* h_recv_hi = nullptr;  // from next -> halo plane nzl+1\n\n    void init(const Domain& dom) {\n        d = &dom;\n        const size_t bytes = dom.plane() * sizeof(double);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send_lo, bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send_hi, bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv_lo, bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv_hi, bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    }\n    void destroy() {\n        cudaFreeHost(h_send_lo); cudaFreeHost(h_send_hi);\n        cudaFreeHost(h_recv_lo); cudaFreeHost(h_recv_hi);\n    }\n\n    void exchange(double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) const {\n        if (d->nzl == 0) return;\n        const size_t plane = d->plane();\n        const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n        const int cnt = static_cast<int>(plane);\n\n        // Stage the boundary planes to the host\n        if (d->prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_lo, dev_field + plane, bytes,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        if (d->next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send_hi, dev_field + plane * d->nzl, bytes,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        MPI_Request reqs[4];\n        int nreq = 0;\n        if (d->prev != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(h_recv_lo, cnt, MPI_DOUBLE, d->prev, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]));\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(h_send_lo, cnt, MPI_DOUBLE, d->prev, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]));\n        }\n        if (d->next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(h_recv_hi, cnt, MPI_DOUBLE, d->next, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]));\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(h_send_hi, cnt, MPI_DOUBLE, d->next, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]));\n        }\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));\n\n        if (d->prev != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dev_field, h_recv_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        if (d->next != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dev_field + plane * (d->nzl + 1), h_recv_hi, bytes,\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    }\n};\n\n// Initialize concentration field (own slab only, same formula as the original)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const Domain& d) {\n    const size_t nx = d.nx, ny = d.ny;\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * d.nz;\n    const size_t plane = d.plane();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (int zl = 0; zl < d.nzl; ++zl) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t z = d.z_off + zl;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(zl + 1), nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    (void)plane;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i]);\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nstatic void selectDevice() {\n    MPI_Comm local;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local));\n    int local_rank = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local, &local_rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&local));\n    int ndev = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&ndev));\n    if (ndev == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % ndev));\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks));\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    selectDevice();\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"Parallelization: MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d, CUDA device per rank\\n\",\n               nranks, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    const Domain dom = makeDomain(nx, ny, nz);\n    const size_t plane = dom.plane();\n\n    // Allocate arrays (local slab + 2 halo planes)\n    std::vector<double> h_c(dom.storageElems(), 0.0);\n    double *d_cold = nullptr, *d_cnew = nullptr, *d_mu = nullptr;\n    const size_t storageBytes = std::max<size_t>(dom.storageElems(), 1) * sizeof(double);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, storageBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, storageBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, storageBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_cold, 0, storageBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_cnew, 0, storageBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_mu, 0, storageBytes));\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(h_c, dom);\n    if (dom.nzl > 0)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cold, h_c.data(), dom.storageElems() * sizeof(double),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    cudaStream_t compStream, commStream;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compStream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&commStream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    cudaEvent_t evBoundaryDone, evHaloDone;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evBoundaryDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evHaloDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n    HaloExchanger halo;\n    halo.init(dom);\n\n    StencilParams sp;\n    sp.nx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    sp.ny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    sp.nzl = dom.nzl;\n    sp.has_prev = (dom.prev != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 0;\n    sp.has_next = (dom.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 0;\n    sp.idx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);\n    sp.idy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);\n    sp.idz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);\n    const double dtD = dt * D;\n\n    // Interior planes [1, nzl-1) can be computed while halos are in flight;\n    // boundary planes 0 and nzl-1 need the halos.\n    const int nzl = dom.nzl;\n    const int intBeg = 1, intEnd = std::max(1, nzl - 1);\n    const bool hasInterior = (intEnd > intBeg);\n    const dim3 block(BLOCK_X, BLOCK_Y, 1);\n    const dim3 gridInterior = gridFor(sp, intBeg, intEnd);\n    const dim3 gridOne = gridFor(sp, 0, 1);\n\n    // Runs `launch(z0, z1)` on the boundary planes of the slab.\n    auto forBoundary = [&](auto&& launch) {\n        if (nzl <= 0) return;\n        launch(0, 1);\n        if (nzl > 1) launch(nzl - 1, nzl);\n    };\n\n    auto launchMu = [&](int z0, int z1, dim3 grid) {\n        chemicalPotentialKernel<<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1,\n                                                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n    auto launchUpdate = [&](int z0, int z1, dim3 grid) {\n        updateKernel<<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(d_cnew, d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1, dtD);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n\n    // Run simulation\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        // --- Chemical potential --------------------------------------------\n        // Halo exchange of c overlaps with the interior mu computation.\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evBoundaryDone, compStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(commStream, evBoundaryDone, 0));\n        if (hasInterior) launchMu(intBeg, intEnd, gridInterior);\n        halo.exchange(d_cold, commStream);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evHaloDone, commStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(compStream, evHaloDone, 0));\n        forBoundary([&](int z0, int z1) { launchMu(z0, z1, gridOne); });\n\n        // --- Concentration update ------------------------------------------\n        // Halo exchange of mu overlaps with the interior update.\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evBoundaryDone, compStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(commStream, evBoundaryDone, 0));\n        if (hasInterior) launchUpdate(intBeg, intEnd, gridInterior);\n        halo.exchange(d_mu, commStream);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evHaloDone, commStream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(compStream, evHaloDone, 0));\n        forBoundary([&](int z0, int z1) { launchUpdate(z0, z1, gridOne); });\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(commStream));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", static_cast<long>(duration.count()));\n\n        // Calculate performance\n        double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        // Gather the full field on rank 0 (own planes only, halos stripped)\n        if (dom.nzl > 0)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_c.data() + plane, d_cold + plane,\n                                  dom.localElems() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        std::vector<double> full;\n        std::vector<int> rcounts(nranks), rdispls(nranks);\n        if (isRoot) {\n            full.resize(gridSize);\n            // Gatherv counts/displacements in elements. Fall back to a\n            // plane-by-plane gather when they would overflow int.\n        }\n        bool fitsInt = true;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n            const size_t cnt = static_cast<size_t>(dom.counts[r]) * plane;\n            const size_t dsp = static_cast<size_t>(dom.displs[r]) * plane;\n            if (cnt > static_cast<size_t>(INT32_MAX) || dsp > static_cast<size_t>(INT32_MAX)) fitsInt = false;\n            rcounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n            rdispls[r] = static_cast<int>(dsp);\n        }\n        if (fitsInt) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(h_c.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(dom.localElems()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                                  isRoot ? full.data() : nullptr, rcounts.data(), rdispls.data(),\n                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n        } else {\n            MPI_Datatype planeType;\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, &planeType));\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_commit(&planeType));\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(h_c.data() + plane, dom.nzl, planeType,\n                                  isRoot ? full.data() : nullptr, dom.counts.data(),\n                                  dom.displs.data(), planeType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_free(&planeType));\n        }\n\n        if (isRoot) {\n            // Print results for external validation\n            if (printResults) {\n                print_results(full, \"Concentration\");\n            }\n\n            // Validation\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                bool valid = validateResult(full, nx, ny, nz);\n\n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    }\n\n    halo.destroy();\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(evBoundaryDone));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(evHaloDone));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(commStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncat > cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.18)\nproject(cahn_hilliard_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX When UNIT_SPACING is set the grid\n// spacing is exactly 1.0, so the (exact) multiplications by 1/h^2 are skipped.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleBy(const double v, const double inv_h2) {\n    return UNIT_SPACING ? v : v * inv_h2;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on local planes [z0, z1).\n// Each thread owns one (x, y) column and marches along z keeping the three\n// z-planes of the centre column in registers; x/y neighbours come from the\n// (L1-cached) global loads of the same plane.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\nchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n                        const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n                        const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n\n    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // storage plane of first cell and of its lower z neighbour (clamped)\n    const int zs0 = zbeg + 1;\n    const double* row = c + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n    double* out = mu + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n    double cv = row[0];\n\n    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : cv;\n\n        const double cxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idx2);\n        const double cyy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idy2);\n        const double czz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * cv, p.idz2);\n\n        *out = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n               - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        below = cv;\n        cv = above;\n        row += plane;\n        out += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu) on local planes [z0, z1)\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\nupdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n             const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n             const double dtD) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n\n    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    const int zs0 = zbeg + 1;\n    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n    const double* row = mu + base;\n    const double* in = cold + base;\n    double* out = cnew + base;\n    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n    double mv = row[0];\n\n    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : mv;\n\n        const double mxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idx2);\n        const double myy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idy2);\n        const double mzz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * mv, p.idz2);\n\n        *out = *in + dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        below = mv;\n        mv = above;\n        row += plane;\n        in += plane;\n        out += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:start] + new + s[end:]\nold_launch = s[s.index(\"    auto launchMu = [&]\"):s.index(\"    // Run simulation\")]\nnew_launch = '''    // Grid spacing of exactly 1.0 lets the kernels skip the (exact) scaling\n    // multiplications of the Laplacian terms.\n    const bool unitSpacing = (sp.idx2 == 1.0 && sp.idy2 == 1.0 && sp.idz2 == 1.0);\n\n    auto launchMu = [&](int z0, int z1, dim3 grid) {\n        if (unitSpacing)\n            chemicalPotentialKernel<true><<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(\n                d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        else\n            chemicalPotentialKernel<false><<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(\n                d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n    auto launchUpdate = [&](int z0, int z1, dim3 grid) {\n        if (unitSpacing)\n            updateKernel<true><<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(d_cnew, d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1, dtD);\n        else\n            updateKernel<false><<<grid, block, 0, compStream>>>(d_cnew, d_cold, d_mu, sp, z0, z1, dtD);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    };\n\n'''\ns = s.replace(old_launch, new_launch)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nsed -i 's/-O3 -fmad=false -Xcompiler/-O3 -Xcompiler/' CMakeLists.txt\ncmake --build build 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\"; D2H/H2D copies are staged through pinned host buffers; a side's\n// copies run on its own CUDA stream.\nstruct HaloExchanger {\n    enum Side { LO = 0, HI = 1 };\n    const Domain* d = nullptr;\n    double* h_send[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // own boundary planes -> neighbour\n    double* h_recv[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // neighbour planes -> halo planes\n    MPI_Request recvReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n    MPI_Request sendReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    void init(const Domain& dom) {\n        d = &dom;\n        const size_t bytes = HALO * dom.plane() * sizeof(double);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        }\n    }\n    void destroy() {\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) { cudaFreeHost(h_send[i]); cudaFreeHost(h_recv[i]); }\n    }\n\n    int neighbour(Side s) const { return s == LO ? d->prev : d->next; }\n    bool active(Side s) const { return d->nzl > 0 && neighbour(s) != MPI_PROC_NULL; }\n    size_t bytes() const { return HALO * d->plane() * sizeof(double); }\n    int count() const { return static_cast<int>(HALO * d->plane()); }\n    // storage offset of the own planes sent to / halo planes received from side s\n    size_t sendOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? 58\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    59\t// CUDA kernels\n    60\t//\n    61\t// Local storage layout on each rank: planes 0 .. nzl+3. Planes 0,1 and\n    62\t// nzl+2,nzl+3 are depth-2 halo planes received from the neighbouring ranks and\n    63\t// planes 2 .. nzl+1 hold the rank's own slab (local plane k is stored at k+2).\n    64\t// The chemical potential is additionally computed on the first halo plane of\n    65\t// each side (local planes -1 and nzl) so that a single exchange of c per time\n    66\t// step suffices for both stencil passes.\n    67\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    68\tnamespace {\n    69\t\n    70\tconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\n    71\tconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n    72\tconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 16;  // planes marched per thread along z\n    73\tconstexpr int HALO = 2;      // halo depth in z (planes per side)\n    74\t\n    75\tstruct StencilParams {\n    76\t    int nx, ny, nzl;          // local slab dimensions (nzl = own planes)\n    77\t    int has_prev, has_next;   // 1 if a neighbour rank exists in that z direction\n    78\t    double idx2, idy2, idz2;  // 1/dx^2, 1/dy^2, 1/dz^2\n    79\t};\n    80\t\n    81\t// 7-point Laplacian contribution helper. When UNIT_SPACING is set the grid\n    82\t// spacing is exactly 1.0, so the (exact) multiplications by 1/h^2 are skipped.\n    83\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n    84\t__device__ __forceinline__ (L1-cached) global loads of the same plane.\n    92\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n    93\t__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\n    94\tchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n   110\t    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n   111\t    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n   112\t\n   113\t    // storage plane of first cell and of its lower z neighbour (clamped)\n   114\t    const int zs0 = zbeg + HALO;\n   115\t    const double* row = c + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n   116\t    double* out = mu + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n   117\t    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n   118\t    double cv = row[0];\n   119\t\n   120\t    for const double czz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * cv, p.idz2);\n   127\t\n   128\t        *out = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n   129\t               +       row += plane;\n   181\t        in += plane;\n   182\t        out += plane;\n   183\t    }\n   184\t}\n   185\t\n   186\tinline dim3 gridFor(const StencilParams& p, const int z0, const int z1) 190\t}\n   191\t\n   192\t}  // namespace\n   193\t\n   194\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   195\t// Distributed simulation state\n   196\t// 215\t    d.counts.resize(d.size);\n   216\t    d.displs.resize(d.size);\n   217\t    // Every active rank owns at least HALO planes so that the depth-2 halo of a\n   218\t    // neighbour is always 237\t// Halo exchange of the first / last HALO own planes of a field with the\n   238\t// neighbouring ranks. The two sides are handled independently so that the\n   239\t// boundary computation of one side can start while the other side is still in\n   240\t// flight. D2H/H2D copies are staged through pinned host buffers; a side's\n   241\t// copies run on its own CUDA stream.\n   242\tstruct HaloExchanger {\n   243\t    enum Side { LO = 0, HI = 1 };\n   244\t    const Domain* d = nullptr;\n   245\t    double* h_send[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // own boundary planes -> neighbour\n   246\t    double* h_recv[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // neighbour planes -> halo planes\n   247\t    MPI_Request recvReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n   248\t    MPI_Request sendReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n   249\t\n   250\t    void init(const Domain& dom) {\n   251\t        d = &dom;\n   252\t        const size_t bytes = HALO * dom.plane() * sizeof(double);\n   253\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n   254\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n   255\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n   256\t        }\n   257\t    }\n   258\t    void destroy() {\n   259\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) { cudaFreeHost(h_send[i]); cudaFreeHost(h_recv[i]); }\n   260\t    }\n   261\t\n   262\t    int neighbour(Side s) const { return s == LO ? d->prev : d->next; }\n   263\t    bool active(Side s) const { return d->nzl > 0 && neighbour(s) != MPI_PROC_NULL; }\n   264\t    size_t bytes() const { return HALO * d->plane() * sizeof(double); }\n   265\t    int count() const { return static_cast<int>(HALO * d->plane()); }\n   266\t    // storage offset of the own planes sent to / halo planes received from side s\n   267\t    size_t sendOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? HALO * d->plane() : d->plane() * d->nzl; }\n   268\t    size_t recvOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? 0 : d->plane() * (d->nzl + HALO); }\n   269\t\n   270\t    // Post the receive and start staging the own boundary planes to the host.\n   271\t    void start(Side s, const double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) {\n   272\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n   273\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(h_recv[s], count(), MPI_DOUBLE, neighbour(s), 1 - s, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n   274\t                            &recvReq[s]));\n   275\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send[s],                                                                     \\\n45\t    45\t        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                                             \\\n46\t    46\t            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d (code %d)\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,     \\\n47\t    47\t                    err__); Planes 0,1 and\n62\t    62\t// nzl+2,nzl+3 are depth-2 halo planes received from the neighbouring ranks and\n63\t    63\t// planes 2 .. nzl+1 hold the rank's own slab (local plane k is stored at k+2).\n64\t    64\t// The chemical potential is additionally computed on the first halo plane of\n65\t    65\t// each side (local planes -1 and nzl) so that a single exchange of c per time\n66\t    66\t// step suffices for both stencil passes.\n67\t    67\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n68\t    68\tnamespace {\n69\t    69\t\n70\t    70\tconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\n71\t    71\tconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n72\t    72\tconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 16;  // planes marched per thread along z\n73\t    73\tconstexpr int HALO = 2;      // halo depth in z (planes per side)\n74\t    74\t\n75\t    75\tstruct StencilParams {\n76\t    76\t    int nx, ny, nzl;          // local slab dimensions (nzl = own planes)\n77\t    77\t    int has_prev, has_next;   // 1 if a neighbour rank exists in that z direction\n78\t    78\t    double idx2, idy2, idz2;  // 1/dx^2, 1/dy^2, 1/dz^2\n79\t    79\t};\n80\t    80\t\n81\t    81\t// 7-point Laplacian contribution helper. When UNIT_SPACING is set the grid\n82\t    82\t// spacing is exactly 1.0, so the (exact) multiplications by 1/h^2 are skipped.\n83\t    83\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n84\t    84\t__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleBy(const double v, const double inv_h2) {\n85\t    85\t    return UNIT_SPACING ? v : v * inv_h2;\n86\t    86\t}\n87\t    87\t\n88\t    88\t// Chemical potential: mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on local planes [z0, z1).\n89\t    89\t// Each thread owns one (x, y) column and marches along z keeping the three\n90\t    90\t// z-planes of the centre column in registers; x/y neighbours come from the\n91\t    91\t// (L1-cached) global loads of the same plane.\n92\t    92\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n93\t    93\t__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\n94\t    94\tchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n95\t    95\t                        const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n96\t    96\t                        const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n97\t    97\t                        const double e_AB) {\n98\t    98\t    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n99\t    99\t    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n100\t   100\t    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n101\t   101\t\n102\t   102\t    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n103\t   103\t    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n104\t   104\t    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n105\t   105\t\n106\t   106\t    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n107\t   107\t    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n108\t   108\t    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n109\t   109\t    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n110\t   110\t    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n111\t   111\t    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n112\t   112\t\n113\t   113\t    // storage plane of first cell and of its lower z neighbour (clamped)\n114\t   114\t    const int zs0 = zbeg + HALO;\n115\t   115\t    const double* row = c + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n116\t   116\t    double* out = mu + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n117\t   117\t    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n118\t   118\t    double cv = row[0];\n119\t   119\t\n120\t   120\t    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n121\t   121\t        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n122\t   122\t        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : cv;\n123\t   123\t\n124\t   124\t        const double cxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idx2);\n125\t   125\t        const double cyy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idy2);\n126\t   126\t        const double czz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * cv, p.idz2);\n127\t   127\t\n128\t   128\t        *out = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n129\t   129\t               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n130\t   130\t               - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n131\t   131\t\n132\t   132\t        below = cv;\n133\t   133\t        cv = above;\n134\t   134\t        row += plane;\n135\t   135\t        out += plane;\n136\t   136\t    }\n137\t   137\t}\n138\t   138\t\n139\t   139\t// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu) on local planes [z0, z1)\n140\t   140\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n141\t   141\t__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\n142\t   142\tupdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n143\t   143\t             const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n144\t   144\t             const double dtD) {\n145\t   145\t    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n146\t   146\t    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n147\t   147\t    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n148\t   148\t\n149\t   149\t    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n150\t   150\t    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n151\t   151\t    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n152\t   152\t\n153\t   153\t    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n154\t   154\t    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n155\t   155\t    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n156\t   156\t    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n157\t   157\t    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n158\t   158\t    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n159\t   159\t\n160\t   160\t    const int zs0 = zbeg + HALO;\n161\t   161\t    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n162\t   162\t    const double* row = mu + base;\n163\t   163\t    const double* in = cold + base;\n164\t   164\t    double* out = cnew + base;\n165\t   165\t    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n166\t   166\t    double mv = row[0];\n167\t   167\t\n168\t   168\t    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n169\t   169\t        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n170\t   170\t        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : mv;\n171\t   171\t\n172\t   172\t        const double mxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idx2);\n173\t   173\t        const double myy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idy2);\n174\t   174\t        const double mzz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * mv, p.idz2);\n175\t   175\t\n176\t   176\t        *out = *in + dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n177\t   177\t\n178\t   178\t        below = mv;\n179\t   179\t        mv = above;\n180\t   180\t        row += plane;\n181\t   181\t        in += plane;\n182\t   182\t        out += plane;\n183\t   183\t    }\n184\t   184\t}\n185\t   185\t\n186\t   186\tinline dim3 gridFor(const StencilParams& p, const int z0, const int z1) {\n187\t   187\t    const int nz = std::max(0, z1 - z0);\n188\t   188\t    return dim3((p.nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X, (p.ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,\n189\t   189\t                (nz + Z_CHUNK - 1) / Z_CHUNK);\n190\t   190\t}\n191\t   191\t\n192\t   192\t}  // namespace\n193\t   193\t\n194\t   194\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n195\t   195\t// Distributed simulation state\n196\t   196\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n197\t   197\tstruct Domain {\n198\t   198\t    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n199\t   199\t    size_t nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;\n200\t   200\t    int nzl = 0;         // own planes on this rank\n201\t   201\t    size_t z_off = 0;    // global index of first own plane\n202\t   202\t    int prev = MPI_PROC_NULL, next = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n203\t   203\t    std::vector<int> counts, displs;  // planes per rank / first plane (for gather)\n204\t   204\t\n205\t   205\t    size_t plane() const { return nx * ny; }\n206\t   206\t    size_t localElems() const { return plane() * nzl; }\n207\t   207\t    size_t storageElems() const { return plane() * (nzl + 2 * HALO); }\n208\t   208\t};\n209\t   209\t\n210\t   210\tstatic Domain makeDomain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n211\t   211\t    Domain d;\n212\t   212\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank));\n213\t   213\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size));\n214\t   214\t    d.nx = nx; d.ny = ny; d.nz = nz;\n215\t   215\t    d.counts.resize(d.size);\n216\t   216\t    d.displs.resize(d.size);\n217\t   217\t    // Every active rank owns at least HALO planes so that the depth-2 halo of a\n218\t   218\t    // neighbour is always made of planes owned by that neighbour.\n219\t   219\t    const int active = (nz >= static_cast<size_t>(HALO))\n220\t   220\t                           ? static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(d.size, nz / HALO)) : 1;\n221\t   221\t    size_t acc = 0;\n222\t   222\t    for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n223\t   223\t        size_t cnt = 0;\n224\t   224\t        if (r < active) cnt = nz / active + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < nz % active ? 1 : 0);\n225\t   225\t        d.counts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n226\t   226\t        d.displs[r] = static_cast<int>(acc);\n227\t   227\t        acc += cnt;\n228\t   228\t    }\n229\t   229\t    d.nzl = d.counts[d.rank];\n230\t   230\t    d.z_off = d.displs[d.rank];\n231\t   231\t    // Nearest neighbours that actually own planes (ranks with 0 planes are skipped)\n232\t   232\t    for (int r = d.rank - 1; r >= 0; --r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.prev = r; break; }\n233\t   233\t    for (int r = d.rank + 1; r < d.size; ++r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.next = r; break; }\n234\t   234\t    return d;\n235\t   235\t}\n236\t   236\t\n237\t   237\t// Halo exchange of the first / last HALO own planes of a field with the\n238\t   238\t// neighbouring ranks. The two sides are handled independently so that the\n239\t   239\t// boundary computation of one side can start while the other side is still in\n240\t   240\t// flight. D2H/H2D copies are staged through pinned host buffers; a side's\n241\t   241\t// copies run on its own CUDA stream.\n242\t   242\tstruct HaloExchanger {\n243\t   243\t    enum Side { LO = 0, HI = 1 };\n244\t   244\t    const Domain* d = nullptr;\n245\t   245\t    double* h_send[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // own boundary planes -> neighbour\n246\t   246\t    double* h_recv[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // neighbour planes -> halo planes\n247\t   247\t    MPI_Request recvReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n248\t   248\t    MPI_Request sendReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n249\t   249\t\n250\t   250\t    void init(const Domain& dom) {\n251\t   251\t        d = &dom;\n252\t   252\t        const size_t bytes = HALO * dom.plane() * sizeof(double);\n253\t   253\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n254\t   254\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n255\t   255\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n256\t   256\t        }\n257\t   257\t    }\n258\t   258\t    void destroy() {\n259\t   259\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) { cudaFreeHost(h_send[i]); cudaFreeHost(h_recv[i]); }\n260\t   260\t    }\n261\t   261\t\n262\t   262\t    int neighbour(Side s) const { return s == LO ? d->prev : d->next; }\n263\t   263\t    bool active(Side s) const { return d->nzl > 0 && neighbour(s) != MPI_PROC_NULL; }\n264\t   264\t    size_t bytes() const { return HALO * d->plane() * sizeof(double); }\n265\t   265\t    int count() const { return static_cast<int>(HALO * d->plane()); }\n266\t   266\t    // storage offset of the own planes sent to / halo planes received from side s\n267\t   267\t    size_t sendOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? HALO * d->plane() : d->plane() * d->nzl; }\n268\t   268\t    size_t recvOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? 0 : d->plane() * (d->nzl + HALO); }\n269\t   269\t\n270\t   270\t    // Post the receive and start staging the own boundary planes to the host.\n271\t   271\t    void start(Side s, const double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) {\n272\t   272\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n273\t   273\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(h_recv[s], count(), MPI_DOUBLE, neighbour(s), 1 - s, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n274\t   274\t                            &recvReq[s]));\n275\t   275\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send[s], dev_field + sendOffset(s), bytes(),\n276\t   276\t                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n277\t   277\t    }\n278\t   278\t    // Wait for the staging copy and send the planes.\n279\t   279\t    void send(Side s, cudaStream_t stream) {\n280\t   280\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n281\t   281\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n282\t   282\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(h_send[s], count(), MPI_DOUBLE, neighbour(s), s, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n283\t   283\t                            &sendReq[s]));\n284\t   284\t    }\n285\t   285\t    // Wait for the neighbour's planes and copy them into the halo.\n286\t   286\t    void receive(Side s, double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) {\n287\t   287\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n288\t   288\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Wait(&recvReq[s], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n289\t   289\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dev_field + recvOffset(s), h_recv[s], bytes(),\n290\t   290\t                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n291\t   291\t    }\n292\t   292\t    void finish() {\n293\t   293\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(2, sendReq, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));\n294\t   294\t    }\n295\t   295\t};\n296\t   296\t\n297\t   297\t// Initialize concentration field (own slab only, same formula as the original)\n298\t   298\tvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const Domain& d) {\n299\t   299\t    const size_t nx = d.nx, ny = d.ny;\n300\t   300\t    const size_t vol = nx * ny * d.nz;\n301\t   301\t\n302\t   302\t#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n303\t   303\t    for (int zl = 0; zl < d.nzl; ++zl) {\n304\t   304\t        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n305\t   305\t            const size_t z = d.z_off + zl;\n306\t   306\t            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n307\t   307\t                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(zl + HALO), nx, ny);\n308\t   308\t                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n309\t   309\t                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n310\t   310\t                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n311\t   311\t                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n312\t   312\t            }\n313\t   313\t        }\n314\t   314\t    }\n315\t   315\t}\n316\t   316\t\n317\t   317\tbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx,\n318\t   318\t                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n319\t   319\t    // Check for NaN or Inf\n320\t   320\t    bool bad = false;\n321\t   321\t#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n322\t   322\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n323\t   323\t        bad = bad || std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i]);\n324\t   324\t    }\n325\t   325\t    if (bad) {\n326\t   326\t        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n327\t   327\t        return false;\n328\t   328\t    }\n329\t   329\t\n330\t   330\t    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n331\t   331\t    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n332\t   332\t    double minVal = c[0];\n333\t   333\t    double maxVal = c[0];\n334\t   334\t#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n335\t   335\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n336\t   336\t        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n337\t   337\t        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n338\t   338\t    }\n339\t   339\t\n340\t   340\t    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n341\t   341\t\n342\t   342\t    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n343\t   343\t    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n344\t   344\t        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n345\t   345\t        return false;\n346\t   346\t    }\n347\t   347\t\n348\t   348\t    return true;\n349\t   349\t}\n350\t   350\t\n351\t   351\tvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n352\t   352\t    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n353\t   353\t    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n354\t   354\t    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n355\t   355\t    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n356\t   356\t    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n357\t   357\t    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n358\t   358\t    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n359\t   359\t    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n360\t   360\t    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n361\t   361\t}\n362\t   362\t\n363\t   363\tstatic void selectDevice() {\n364\t   364\t    MPI_Comm local;\n365\t   365\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local));\n366\t   366\t    int local_rank = 0;\n367\t   367\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local, &local_rank));\n368\t   368\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&local));\n369\t   369\t    int ndev = 0;\n370\t   370\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&ndev));\n371\t   371\t    if (ndev == 0) {\n372\t   372\t        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n373\t   373\t        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n374\t   374\t    }\n375\t   375\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % ndev));\n376\t   376\t}\n377\t   377\t\n378\t   378\tint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n379\t   379\t    int provided = 0;\n380\t   380\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n381\t   381\t\n382\t   382\t    size_t nx = 64;\n383\t   383\t    size_t ny = 0;\n384\t   384\t    size_t nz = 0;\n385\t   385\t    int iterations = 20;\n386\t   386\t    bool validate = false;\n387\t   387\t    bool printResults = false;\n388\t   388\t\n389\t   389\t    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n390\t   390\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n391\t   391\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks));\n392\t   392\t    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n393\t   393\t\n394\t   394\t    // Parse command line arguments\n395\t   395\t    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n396\t   396\t        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n397\t   397\t            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n398\t   398\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n399\t   399\t            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n400\t   400\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n401\t   401\t            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n402\t   402\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n403\t   403\t            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n404\t   404\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n405\t   405\t            validate = true;\n406\t   406\t        } else if     1\t// Cahn-Hilliard phase separation benchmark\n     2\t//\n     3\t// Hybrid parallelization:\n     4\t//   * MPI    : 1D slab decomposition of the 3D grid along Z, one rank per GPU,\n     5\t//              halo exchange of boundary planes every half step, overlapped with\n     6\t//              the interior stencil computation on the GPU.\n     7\t//   * CUDA   : the chemical-potential and concentration-update stencils run on\n     8\t//              the GPU (register-marching 7-point stencil kernels, two streams).\n     9\t//   * OpenMP : host-side work (field initialization, halo buffer handling,\n    10\t//              gather and validation reductions) is multithreaded.\n    11\t//\n    12\t// Semantics (initial field, stencil, clamped boundaries, update, output) are\n    13\t// identical to the original serial implementation.\n    14\t\n    15\t#include <algorithm>\n    16\t#include <chrono>\n    17\t#include <cmath>\n    18\t#include <cstdio>\n    19\t#include
