\"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n    28\t\n    29\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    30\t// Error handling helpers\n    31\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    32\t#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                        \\\n    33\t    do {                                                                                        \\\n    34\t        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                             \\\n    35\t        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n    36\t            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,          \\\n    37\t                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                                 \\\n    38\t            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                       \\\n    39\t        }                                                                                       \\\n    40\t    } while (0)\n    41\t\n    42\t#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                                         \\\n    43\t    do {                                                                                        \\\n    44\t        int err__ = (call);                                                                     \\\n    45\t        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                                             \\\n    46\t            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d (code %d)\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,     \\\n    47\t                    err__);                                                                     \\\n    48\t            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                       \\\n    49\t        }                                                                                       \\\n    50\t    } while (0)\n    51\t\n    52\t// 3D index calculation\n    53\t__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,\n    54\t                                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    55\t    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n    56\t}\n    57\t\n    58\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    59\t// CUDA kernels\n    60\t//\n    61\t// Local storage layout on each rank: planes 0 .. nzl+3. Planes 0,1 and\n    62\t// nzl+2,nzl+3 are depth-2 halo planes received from the neighbouring ranks and\n    63\t// planes 2 .. nzl+1 hold the rank's own slab (local plane k is stored at k+2).\n    64\t// The chemical potential is additionally computed on the first halo plane of\n    65\t// each side (local planes -1 and nzl) so that a single exchange of c per time\n    66\t// step suffices for both stencil passes.\n    67\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    68\tnamespace {\n    69\t\n    70\tconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\n    71\tconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n    72\tconstexpr int Z_CHUNK = 16;  // planes marched per thread along z\n    73\tconstexpr int HALO = 2;      // halo depth in z (planes per side)\n    74\t\n    75\tstruct StencilParams {\n    76\t    int nx, ny, nzl;          // local slab dimensions (nzl = own planes)\n    77\t    int has_prev, has_next;   // 1 if a neighbour rank exists in that z direction\n    78\t    double idx2, idy2, idz2;  // 1/dx^2, 1/dy^2, 1/dz^2\n    79\t};\n    80\t\n    81\t// 7-point Laplacian contribution helper. When UNIT_SPACING is set the grid\n    82\t// spacing is exactly 1.0, so the (exact) multiplications by 1/h^2 are skipped.\n    83\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n    84\t__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleBy(const double v, const double inv_h2) {\n    85\t    return UNIT_SPACING ? v : v * inv_h2;\n    86\t}\n    87\t\n    88\t// Chemical potential: mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c) on local planes [z0, z1).\n    89\t// Each thread owns one (x, y) column and marches along z keeping the three\n    90\t// z-planes of the centre column in registers; x/y neighbours come from the\n    91\t// (L1-cached) global loads of the same plane.\n    92\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n    93\t__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\n    94\tchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    95\t                        const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n    96\t                        const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    97\t                        const double e_AB) {\n    98\t    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    99\t    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n   100\t    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n   101\t\n   102\t    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n   103\t    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n   104\t    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n   105\t\n   106\t    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n   107\t    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n   108\t    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n   109\t    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n   110\t    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n   111\t    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n   112\t\n   113\t    // storage plane of first cell and of its lower z neighbour (clamped)\n   114\t    const int zs0 = zbeg + HALO;\n   115\t    const double* row = c + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n   116\t    double* out = mu + static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n   117\t    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n   118\t    double cv = row[0];\n   119\t\n   120\t    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n   121\t        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n   122\t        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : cv;\n   123\t\n   124\t        const double cxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idx2);\n   125\t        const double cyy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * cv, p.idy2);\n   126\t        const double czz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * cv, p.idz2);\n   127\t\n   128\t        *out = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n   129\t               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n   130\t               - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n   131\t\n   132\t        below = cv;\n   133\t        cv = above;\n   134\t        row += plane;\n   135\t        out += plane;\n   136\t    }\n   137\t}\n   138\t\n   139\t// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu) on local planes [z0, z1)\n   140\ttemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n   141\t__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X * BLOCK_Y)\n   142\tupdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n   143\t             const double* __restrict__ mu, const StencilParams p, const int z0, const int z1,\n   144\t             const double dtD) {\n   145\t    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n   146\t    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n   147\t    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n   148\t\n   149\t    const int zbeg = z0 + blockIdx.z * Z_CHUNK;\n   150\t    const int zend = min(zbeg + Z_CHUNK, z1);\n   151\t    if (zbeg >= zend) return;\n   152\t\n   153\t    const int dxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n   154\t    const int dxn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n   155\t    const int dyp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? p.nx : 0;\n   156\t    const int dyn = (y > 0) ? -p.nx : 0;\n   157\t    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * p.ny;\n   158\t    const size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n   159\t\n   160\t    const int zs0 = zbeg + HALO;\n   161\t    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(zs0) * plane + off;\n   162\t    const double* row = mu + base;\n   163\t    const double* in = cold + base;\n   164\t    double* out = cnew + base;\n   165\t    double below = (zbeg > 0 || p.has_prev) ? row[-static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(plane)] : row[0];\n   166\t    double mv = row[0];\n   167\t\n   168\t    for (int zl = zbeg; zl < zend; ++zl) {\n   169\t        const bool hasAbove = (zl < p.nzl - 1 || p.has_next);\n   170\t        const double above = hasAbove ? row[plane] : mv;\n   171\t\n   172\t        const double mxx = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dxp] + row[dxn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idx2);\n   173\t        const double myy = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(row[dyp] + row[dyn] - 2.0 * mv, p.idy2);\n   174\t        const double mzz = scaleBy<UNIT_SPACING>(above + below - 2.0 * mv, p.idz2);\n   175\t\n   176\t        *out = *in + dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n   177\t\n   178\t        below = mv;\n   179\t        mv = above;\n   180\t        row += plane;\n   181\t        in += plane;\n   182\t        out += plane;\n   183\t    }\n   184\t}\n   185\t\n   186\tinline dim3 gridFor(const StencilParams& p, const int z0, const int z1) {\n   187\t    const int nz = std::max(0, z1 - z0);\n   188\t    return dim3((p.nx + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X, (p.ny + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y,\n   189\t                (nz + Z_CHUNK - 1) / Z_CHUNK);\n   190\t}\n   191\t\n   192\t}  // namespace\n   193\t\n   194\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   195\t// Distributed simulation state\n   196\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   197\tstruct Domain {\n   198\t    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n   199\t    size_t nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;\n   200\t    int nzl = 0;         // own planes on this rank\n   201\t    size_t z_off = 0;    // global index of first own plane\n   202\t    int prev = MPI_PROC_NULL, next = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n   203\t    std::vector<int> counts, displs;  // planes per rank / first plane (for gather)\n   204\t\n   205\t    size_t plane() const { return nx * ny; }\n   206\t    size_t localElems() const { return plane() * nzl; }\n   207\t    size_t storageElems() const { return plane() * (nzl + 2 * HALO); }\n   208\t};\n   209\t\n   210\tstatic Domain makeDomain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n   211\t    Domain d;\n   212\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank));\n   213\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size));\n   214\t    d.nx = nx; d.ny = ny; d.nz = nz;\n   215\t    d.counts.resize(d.size);\n   216\t    d.displs.resize(d.size);\n   217\t    // Every active rank owns at least HALO planes so that the depth-2 halo of a\n   218\t    // neighbour is always made of planes owned by that neighbour.\n   219\t    const int active = (nz >= static_cast<size_t>(HALO))\n   220\t                           ? static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(d.size, nz / HALO)) : 1;\n   221\t    size_t acc = 0;\n   222\t    for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n   223\t        size_t cnt = 0;\n   224\t        if (r < active) cnt = nz / active + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < nz % active ? 1 : 0);\n   225\t        d.counts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n   226\t        d.displs[r] = static_cast<int>(acc);\n   227\t        acc += cnt;\n   228\t    }\n   229\t    d.nzl = d.counts[d.rank];\n   230\t    d.z_off = d.displs[d.rank];\n   231\t    // Nearest neighbours that actually own planes (ranks with 0 planes are skipped)\n   232\t    for (int r = d.rank - 1; r >= 0; --r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.prev = r; break; }\n   233\t    for (int r = d.rank + 1; r < d.size; ++r) if (d.counts[r] > 0) { d.next = r; break; }\n   234\t    return d;\n   235\t}\n   236\t\n   237\t// Halo exchange of the first / last HALO own planes of a field with the\n   238\t// neighbouring ranks. The two sides are handled independently so that the\n   239\t// boundary computation of one side can start while the other side is still in\n   240\t// flight. D2H/H2D copies are staged through pinned host buffers; a side's\n   241\t// copies run on its own CUDA stream.\n   242\tstruct HaloExchanger {\n   243\t    enum Side { LO = 0, HI = 1 };\n   244\t    const Domain* d = nullptr;\n   245\t    double* h_send[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // own boundary planes -> neighbour\n   246\t    double* h_recv[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // neighbour planes -> halo planes\n   247\t    MPI_Request recvReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n   248\t    MPI_Request sendReq[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n   249\t\n   250\t    void init(const Domain& dom) {\n   251\t        d = &dom;\n   252\t        const size_t bytes = HALO * dom.plane() * sizeof(double);\n   253\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n   254\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_send[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n   255\t            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_recv[i], bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault));\n   256\t        }\n   257\t    }\n   258\t    void destroy() {\n   259\t        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) { cudaFreeHost(h_send[i]); cudaFreeHost(h_recv[i]); }\n   260\t    }\n   261\t\n   262\t    int neighbour(Side s) const { return s == LO ? d->prev : d->next; }\n   263\t    bool active(Side s) const { return d->nzl > 0 && neighbour(s) != MPI_PROC_NULL; }\n   264\t    size_t bytes() const { return HALO * d->plane() * sizeof(double); }\n   265\t    int count() const { return static_cast<int>(HALO * d->plane()); }\n   266\t    // storage offset of the own planes sent to / halo planes received from side s\n   267\t    size_t sendOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? HALO * d->plane() : d->plane() * d->nzl; }\n   268\t    size_t recvOffset(Side s) const { return s == LO ? 0 : d->plane() * (d->nzl + HALO); }\n   269\t\n   270\t    // Post the receive and start staging the own boundary planes to the host.\n   271\t    void start(Side s, const double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) {\n   272\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n   273\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(h_recv[s], count(), MPI_DOUBLE, neighbour(s), 1 - s, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n   274\t                            &recvReq[s]));\n   275\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send[s], dev_field + sendOffset(s), bytes(),\n   276\t                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n   277\t    }\n   278\t    // Wait for the staging copy and send the planes.\n   279\t    void send(Side s, cudaStream_t stream) {\n   280\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n   281\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n   282\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(h_send[s], count(), MPI_DOUBLE, neighbour(s), s, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n   283\t                            &sendReq[s]));\n   284\t    }\n   285\t    // Wait for the neighbour's planes and copy them into the halo.\n   286\t    void receive(Side s, double* dev_field, cudaStream_t stream) {\n   287\t        if (!active(s)) return;\n   288\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Wait(&recvReq[s], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n   289\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dev_field + recvOffset(s), h_recv[s], bytes(),\n   290\t                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n   291\t    }\n   292\t    void finish() {\n   293\t        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(2, sendReq, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));\n   294\t    }\n   295\t};\n   296\t\n   297\t// Initialize concentration field (own slab only, same formula as the original)\n   298\tvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const Domain& d) {\n   299\t    const size_t nx = d.nx, ny = d.ny;\n   300\t    const size_t vol = nx * ny * d.nz;\n   301\t\n   302\t#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n   303\t    for (int zl = 0; zl < d.nzl; ++zl) {\n   304\t        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n   305\t            const size_t z = d.z_off + zl;\n   306\t            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n   307\t                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(zl + HALO), nx, ny);\n   308\t                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n   309\t                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n   310\t                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n   311\t                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n   312\t            }\n   313\t        }\n   314\t    }\n   315\t}\n   316\t\n   317\tbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx,\n   318\t                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n   319\t    // Check for NaN or Inf\n   320\t    bool bad = false;\n   321\t#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n   322\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n   323\t        bad = bad || std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i]);\n   324\t    }\n   325\t    if (bad) {\n   326\t        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n   327\t        return false;\n   328\t    }\n   329\t\n   330\t    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n   331\t    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n   332\t    double minVal = c[0];\n   333\t    double maxVal = c[0];\n   334\t#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n   335\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n   336\t        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n   337\t        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n   338\t    }\n   339\t\n   340\t    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n   341\t\n   342\t    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n   343\t    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n   344\t        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n   345\t        return false;\n   346\t    }\n   347\t\n   348\t    return true;\n   349\t}\n   350\t\n   351\tvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n   352\t    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n   353\t    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n   354\t    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n   355\t    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n   356\t    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n   357\t    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n   358\t    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n   359\t    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n   360\t    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n   361\t}\n   362\t\n   363\tstatic void selectDevice() {\n   364\t    MPI_Comm local;\n   365\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local));\n   366\t    int local_rank = 0;\n   367\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local, &local_rank));\n   368\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&local));\n   369\t    int ndev = 0;\n   370\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&ndev));\n   371\t    if (ndev == 0) {\n   372\t        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n   373\t        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n   374\t    }\n   375\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % ndev));\n   376\t}\n   377\t\n   378\tint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n   379\t    int provided = 0;\n   380\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n   381\t\n   382\t    size_t nx = 64;\n   383\t    size_t ny = 0;\n   384\t    size_t nz = 0;\n   385\t    int iterations = 20;\n   386\t    bool validate = false;\n   387\t    bool printResults = false;\n   388\t\n   389\t    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n   390\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n   391\t    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks));\n   392\t    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n   393\t\n   394\t    // Parse command line arguments\n   395\t    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n   396\t        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n   397\t            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n   398\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n   399\t            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n   400\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n   401\t            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n   402\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n   403\t            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n   404\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n   405\t            validate = true;\n   406\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n   407\t            printResults = true;\n   408\t        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n   409\t            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n   410\t            MPI_Finalize();\n   411\t            return 0;\n   412\t        } else {\n   413\t            if (isRoot) {\n   414\t                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n   415\t                printUsage(argv[0]);\n   416\t            }\n   417\t            MPI_Finalize();\n   418\t            return 1;\n   419\t        }\n   420\t    }\n   421\t\n   422\t    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n   423\t    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n   424\t\n   425\t    selectDevice();\n   426\t\n   427\t    if (isRoot) {\n   428\t        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n   429\t        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n   430\t        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n   431\t        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n   432\t        printf(\"Parallelization: MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d, CUDA device per rank\\n\",\n   433\t               nranks, omp_get_max_threads());\n   434\t    }\n   435\t\n   436\t    // Physical parameters\n   437\t    const double dx = 1.0;\n   438\t    const double dy = 1.0;\n   439\t    const double dz = 1.0;\n   440\t    const double dt = 0.01;\n   441\t    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n   442\t    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n   443\t    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n   444\t    const double gamma = 0.5;\n   445\t    const double D = 1.0;\n   446\t\n   447\t    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n   448\t\n   449\t    const Domain dom = makeDomain(nx, ny, nz);\n   450\t    const size_t plane = dom.plane();\n   451\t\n   452\t    // Allocate arrays (local slab + 2 halo planes)\n   453\t    std::vector<double> h_c(dom.storageElems(), 0.0);\n   454\t    double *d_cold = nullptr, *d_cnew = nullptr, *d_mu = nullptr;\n   455\t    const size_t storageBytes = std::max<size_t>(dom.storageElems(), 1) * sizeof(double);\n   456\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, storageBytes));\n   457\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, storageBytes));\n   458\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, storageBytes));\n   459\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_cold, 0, storageBytes));\n   460\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_cnew, 0, storageBytes));\n   461\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_mu, 0, storageBytes));\n   462\t\n   463\t    // Initialize concentration field\n   464\t    if (isRoot) printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n   465\t    initializeConcentration(h_c, dom);\n   466\t    if (dom.nzl > 0)\n   467\t        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cold, h_c.data(), dom.storageElems() * sizeof(double),\n   468\t                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n   469\t\n   470\t    cudaStream_t compStream, commStream[2];\n   471\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compStream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n   472\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&commStream[0], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n   473\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&commStream[1], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n   474\t    cudaEvent_t evBoundaryDone, evHaloDone[2];\n   475\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evBoundaryDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n   476\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evHaloDone[0], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n   477\t    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evHaloDone[1], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n   478\t\n   479\t    HaloExchanger halo;\n   480\t    halo.init(dom);\n   481\t\n   482\t    StencilParams sp;\n   483\t    sp.nx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n   484\t    sp.ny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n   485\t    sp.nzl = dom.nzl;\n   486\t    sp.has_prev = (dom.prev != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 0;\n   487\t    sp.has_next = (dom.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) ? 1 : 0;\n   488\t    sp.idx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);\n   489\t    sp.idy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);\n   490\t    sp.idz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);\n   491\t    const double dtD = dt * D;\n   492\t\n   493\t    // Plane ranges (local z). The interior ranges only touch own planes and are\n   494\t    // computed while the halo exchange is in flight; the boundary ranges need\n   495\t    // the halos. mu is also computed on the first halo plane of each side.\n   496\t    const int nzl = dom.nzl;\n   497\t    const int hp = sp.has_prev, hn = sp.has_next;\n   498\t    struct Range { int z0, z1; };\n   499\t    const Range muInterior{1, std::max(1, nzl - 1)};\n   500\t    const Range upInterior{2, std::max(2, nzl - 2)};\n   501\t    // Boundary ranges per side; sideRanges[LO] can run once the lower halo has\n   502\t    // arrived, sideRanges[HI] once both have. For small slabs the lower and\n   503\t    // upper boundary regions overlap and are computed together after both.\n   504\t    struct SideWork { std::vector<Range> mu, up; };\n   505\t    SideWork sideWork[2];\n   506\t    if (nzl > 4) {\n   507\t        sideWork[0].mu.push_back({-hp, 1});\n   508\t        sideWork[0].up.push_back({0, 2});\n   509\t        sideWork[1].mu.push_back({nzl - 1, nzl + hn});\n   510\t        sideWork[1].up.push_back({nzl - 2, nzl});\n   511\t    } else if (nzl > 0) {\n   512\t        if (nzl - 1 <= 1) sideWork[1].mu.push_back({-hp, nzl + hn});\n   513\t        else { sideWork[1].mu.push_back({-hp, 1}); sideWork[1].mu.push_back({nzl - 1, nzl + hn}); }\n   514\t        sideWork[1].up.push_back({0, nzl});\n   515\t    }\n   516\t    const dim3 block(BLOCK_X, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgoMKYzSvmR67nTVN4Ce3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The Cahn-Hilliard benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program. Only the two existing files changed, the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`, and no new files remain in the project.\n\n**Design**\n\n- **MPI** splits the grid into z-slabs, one rank per GPU (GPU chosen by node-local rank). Each rank keeps a depth-2 halo of the concentration field and computes the chemical potential on the first halo plane too, so one exchange per time step serves both stencil passes. The decomposition guarantees every active rank owns at least 2 planes, and ranks with no planes are skipped as neighbours, so any grid size works with any rank count.\n- **CUDA** runs both stencils as register-marching kernels (one thread per x/y column walking along z). Interior planes compute while the halo is in flight. The lower and upper halves of the exchange run on separate copy streams, so each side's boundary planes start as soon as that side's halo arrives.\n- **OpenMP** parallelizes the host-side field initialization and the NaN/range validation reductions.\n- Output is gathered to rank 0 and printed through the unchanged `print_results` and validation code. Only rank 0 prints.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nBuilt with FMA contraction disabled on both sides, the GPU program matches the serial CPU code bitwise for 120 combinations of 10 grid shapes and 1 to 4 ranks, including nz smaller than the rank count. The delivered build leaves nvcc's FMA contraction on for speed, so results differ from the original binary only in the last 1 to 2 ulps, which is the same kind of difference the original already has from gcc's own contraction. Validation passes with 1, 2, and 4 ranks.\n\n**Performance on this node** (4x RTX 3090, host-staged halo exchange)\n\n| Grid | Serial original | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 256³ | 168 MCell/s | 8.1 GCell/s | 15.7 GCell/s | 22 GCell/s |\n| 512³ | n/a | 9.2 GCell/s | 17.2 GCell/s | 31.8 GCell/s |\n\nTwo environment limits are worth knowing. These GeForce cards report no peer access and the installed Open MPI is not CUDA-aware, so halos must go through host memory, and that path costs about 360 µs per step regardless of buffer type or thread count. Also, double-precision work on the 3090 is bound by FP64 instruction rate, not memory bandwidth. Both limits disappear on data-centre GPUs with NVLink or CUDA-aware MPI, where the same code should scale closer to linearly on smaller grids too.\n\n**Build notes**\n\nThe CMake file now enables the CUDA language, compiles the `.cpp` as CUDA, links MPI and OpenMP, and targets the build machine's GPU architecture by default. Override with `-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=...` for a different cluster. Run with `mpirun -np <GPUs> ./cahn_hilliard ...`. Nothing is left pending."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1507,"cache_read_input_tokens":126297,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1507},"output_tokens":769,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cbe9bc76-4749-4069-96a6-e69923ed42bc","uuid":"94f2e4c4-db71-4da9-9b54-fe23e7182b91","timestamp":"2026-11-07T09:43:55.209Z","request_id":"req_011CgoMKYoHr3BkvhS6TVSrW"}
